## Stages 294–295 (`1,004/1,004` Unit Tests & Crossing 4,000+ PR Milestone · 512 Zero-Overlap PRs Across 70 Upstream Repositories · 4,230 Cumulative Zero-Overlap PRs!): x402-rs Solana Compute Budget Cap & Nonce Revert, Tempo MPP Stripe `externalId` Challenge Binding & Pending-Close Channel Stop, AP2-Haggle & ERC-8107 Pinned Expiry & Credo-TS DIDComm v2 Routing Keys, UCP Split-Payment Credential Non-Echo & Omit Strip, Visa MPP/VDP SSL & WBA Directory URI, A2A `interrupted` Non-Terminal & JS Single-Oneof & .NET Stream Persistence Error, Stripe Retry Idempotency & CDP Spurious ERC-20 `approve()` Removal, and MCPB Real PKCS#7 Verify & 11-SDK Deterministic Guards & 7-Card Benchmark Report

- **Primary-Source Protocol Coverage (512 Zero-Overlap Upstream PRs Across 70 Repositories, `PR-01..PR-16`)**:
  - `PR-01..PR-02` (`coinbase/x402#382..#374`, `x402-rs/x402-rs#35..#28`, `google-agentic-commerce/a2a-x402#75..#53`, `tempoxyz/mpp#1113..#1110`, `wevm/mppx#1061..#1057`, `tempoxyz/tempo#8627..#8623`, `tempoxyz/mpp-rs#645..#640`, `tempoxyz/mpp-go#245..#241`, `tempoxyz/pympp#334..#330`, `tempoxyz/mpp-specs#380..#378` & `tempoxyz/mpp-tools#292..#290`): Adds configurable Solana compute budget limits, reverts nonce updates on tx errors, and supports settle-before-execution in `x402-rs` (`#31`, `#29`, `#28`) alongside Go x402 v2 and `PaymentReceipt` in `a2a-x402` (`#65`, `#53`); binds Stripe credential `externalId` to challenge requests, stops charging channels with pending close requests, and removes bare `assert` validation in `tempoxyz/mpp-rs` and `pympp` (`mpp-rs#645..#641`, `pympp#334`, `#331`), stops echoing unexpected error text in `mpp-go` (`#242`), and dispatches composed session credentials by currency in `wevm/mppx` (`#1060`).
  - `PR-03..PR-05` (`google-agentic-commerce/AP2#234..#228`, `ChainAgnostic/CAIPs#336..#315`, `ethereum/ERCs#2086..#2080`, `openwallet-foundation/sd-jwt-js#339..#333`, `openwallet-foundation/credo-ts#3114..#3109`, `Universal-Commerce-Protocol/ucp#897..#889`, `ucp-schema#74..#65`, `python-sdk#16..#12`, `js-sdk#39..#34`, `samples#199..#193`, `agentic-commerce-protocol#88..#76`, `cloudflare/web-bot-auth#95..#84`, `visa/ai#13..#7`, `visa/visa-vulnerability-agentic-harness#24..#13` & `visa/acceptance-devices-ios-sdk#12..#9`): Introduces `AP2-Haggle` real-time negotiation and human-present Solana Pay (`AP2#229`, `#228`), `ERC-8107` pinned expiry deadlines (`#2085`), and `credo-ts` unqualified AnonCreds removal and single DIDComm v2 routing keys (`#3114..#3110`); stops echoing credentials in UCP split-payment examples (`ucp#893`), stops crawling external refs in `ucp-schema` (`#72`), and strips client-supplied `omit` members in `samples` (`#196`, `#193`); adds MPP skill support, VDP mutual SSL, and VIC card provisioning in `visa/ai` (`#13..#8`), `visa/acceptance-devices-ios-sdk` `v3.9.1` (`#12..#9`), and `web-bot-auth` `Signature-Agent` parsers and directory URI validation (`#93..#84`).
  - `PR-06..PR-12` (10 A2A Repositories, 12 Stripe Repositories, `coinbase/agentkit#1379..#1355`, `coinbase/cdp-sdk#741..#727`, `sierra-research/tau2-bench#558..#531`, `tau-bench#15..#8` & `mu-bench#37..#32`): Clarifies `interrupted` non-terminal `TaskState` (`A2A#2270`), redacts `--svc-param` in `a2a-cli` (`#83`), and enforces `ITK_SCENARIO_TIMEOUT` (`a2a-itk#75`); rejects multi-arm `Part.fromJSON`, normalizes RFC 3339 UTC, and clones push configs in `a2a-js` (`#662`, `#656`, `#654`) alongside versioned task storage in `a2a-python` (`#1249`, `#1245`); supports tenant `@PublicAgentCard` and push ordering in `a2a-java` (`#1176`, `#1156`) and surfaces streaming persistence failures in `a2a-dotnet` (`#505`); adds agent retry idempotency guards (`agent-toolkit#419`), hardcoded local skill exemptions (`stripe/ai#449`), and checkout context decoupling (`react-stripe-js#713`); removes spurious ERC-20 `approve()` calls and adds account-level x402 signing in `cdp-sdk` (`#741..#729`); and records `###OUT-OF-SCOPE###` aborts distinctly while preserving checkpoints on write failure in `tau2-bench` (`#548`, `#533`).
  - `PR-13..PR-16` (MCP 11-Language SDKs + Spec + MCPB + Use-MCP + Inspector + Registry + Servers): Verifies PKCS#7 signatures and prevents `$-` pattern corruption in `mcpb` (`#259`, `#255`) and returns `-32602` on unknown tools in Python `MCPServer` (`servers#5175`); enforces same-origin redirects, idle session map caps, and explicit `Path` precedence in `typescript-sdk` (`#2914`, `#2902..#2900`) and validates `null` `structuredContent` and `x-mcp-header` annotations in `python-sdk` (`#3621`, `#3620`); strips query params from fallback auth base URLs and rejects legacy requests on 2026-only servers in `go-sdk` (`#1348`, `#1345`) and returns `400 InvalidParams` on invalid `initialize` while filtering client tool calls in `csharp-sdk` (`#1884`, `#1882`); and preserves client responses during polling in `php-sdk` (`#545`), validates legacy AS metadata issuers and omits body `client_id` with HTTP Basic in `ruby-sdk` (`#556`, `#548`), and guards `NetworkTransport` connect continuations against stale reconnections in `swift-sdk` (`#178`).

## Stages 292–293 (Historic `1,000/1,000` Unit Tests Milestone · 512 Zero-Overlap PRs Across 69 Upstream Repositories · 3,718 Cumulative Zero-Overlap PRs!): x402 `SpendingPolicy` Circuit Breaker & Explicit Resource, Tempo MPP Rust/Go/Python Definitive Rejection & Settled Hash Replay `403`, AP2 Normative `checkout_hash` Verifier & ERC-8107 Subnames, UCP Signed `ucp-agent` Coverage, Visa VIC `X-Pay-Token` Lexicographical Query & Order Object-Level Authz, A2A Go Push SSRF/CGNAT Hardening & Python Keyset Cursors, Stripe Multi-SDK `next-major` Gates & CLI Plugin Guard, Coinbase AgentKit x402 Allowlist Prefix & Permit2 EIP-712 Bind, Sierra `tau2-bench` Strict Replay, and MCPB Zip-Bomb & 11-SDK Deterministic Guards & 7-Card Benchmark Report

- **Primary-Source Protocol Coverage (512 Zero-Overlap Upstream PRs Across 69 Repositories, `PR-01..PR-16`)**:
  - `PR-01..PR-02` (`coinbase/x402#394..#384`, `x402-rs/x402-rs#45..#36`, `google-agentic-commerce/a2a-x402#95..#77`, `tempoxyz/mpp#1121..#1114`, `wevm/mppx#1066..#1062`, `tempoxyz/tempo#8632..#8628`, `tempoxyz/mpp-rs#651..#646`, `tempoxyz/mpp-go#252..#246`, `tempoxyz/pympp#339..#335`, `tempoxyz/mpp-specs#383..#381` & `tempoxyz/mpp-tools#295..#293`): Enforces `SpendingPolicy` circuit breakers and explicit payment resources (`a2a-x402#93`, `#90`, `#77`) plus ID-based scheme identification and dynamic pricing (`x402-rs#45..#40`); treats definitive MCP management rejections as final rejections, measures sponsored issuance floors exactly, and answers settled hash replays with `403` (`tempoxyz/mpp-rs#650`, `#649`, `#646`) alongside request-scoped composed handler dispatch (`wevm/mppx#1066`).
  - `PR-03..PR-05` (`google-agentic-commerce/AP2#245..#235`, `ChainAgnostic/CAIPs#356..#344`, `ethereum/ERCs#2094..#2087`, `openwallet-foundation/sd-jwt-js#348..#340`, `Universal-Commerce-Protocol/ucp#907..#898`, `ucp-schema#82..#75`, `python-sdk#21..#17`, `js-sdk#45..#40`, `samples#204..#200`, `agentic-commerce-protocol#102..#89`, `cloudflare/web-bot-auth#104..#96`, `visa/ai#21..#14`, `visa/vic-reference-agent#3..#1` & `visa/visa-vulnerability-agentic-harness#31..#26`): Enforces normative `checkout_hash` verification (`AP2#243`), `CAIP-356` payment URIs, and `ERC-8107` subname continuity (`ERCs#2094`); covers `ucp-agent` in checkout signed requests (`ucp#907`) and closes open composition branches in strict mode (`ucp-schema#77`); **Primary-source Visa repository coverage**: sorts query parameters lexicographically for `X-Pay-Token`, adds AffixIO `ACTION` VIC spend governance, and validates JWKS chains in `visa/ai` (`#21..#16`) while enforcing order object-level authz and tool input sanitization in `visa/vic-reference-agent` (`#3..#1`).
  - `PR-06..PR-12` (10 A2A Repositories, 12 Stripe Repositories, `coinbase/agentkit#1460..#1380`, `coinbase/cdp-sdk#761..#742`, `sierra-research/tau2-bench#577..#562`, `tau-bench#27..#16` & `mu-bench#47..#38`): Treats only exact SSE media types as streams (`a2a-itk#79`) and escapes Inspector JSON (`a2a-inspector#172`); uses keyset cursors and rejects terminal-task operations (`a2a-python#1282`, `#1268`) while checking required extensions before history mutation (`a2a-js#690`); hardens Push SSRF/CGNAT/CRLF (`a2a-go#397`, `#394`) and validates wire status filters and `A2A-Version` (`a2a-dotnet#526`, `#516`); blocks unsolicited plugin auto-upgrades on custom paths or `--help` (`stripe-cli#2052`, `#2050`) and enforces `next-major` breaking targets and `stripe-mock` OpenAPI pins across Stripe SDKs (`#1918`, `#2302`, `#3464`); stops x402 allowlist prefix bypass and binds 0x Permit2 EIP-712 (`agentkit#1394..#1392`); and enforces strict trajectory replay, gold-arg checks, and gift-card budget limits in `tau2-bench` (`#573..#569`).
  - `PR-13..PR-16` (MCP 11-Language SDKs + Spec + MCPB + Use-MCP + Inspector + Registry + Servers): Bounds `mcpb unpack` against decompression bombs and escapes variable regex metacharacters (`#273`, `#269`), uses `BroadcastChannel` and honors `preventAutoAuth` in `use-mcp` (`#35`, `#34`); drains piped `stderr` and refuses second `connect()` / stateless reuse in `typescript-sdk` (`#2921`, `#2918`) and rejects non-preflight `OPTIONS` on body-reading routes in `python-sdk` (`#3658`, `#3630`); answers malformed spec params with `-32602` and decodes floats via `serde_json::Number` in `rust-sdk` (`#1303`, `#1300`) and checks declared client capabilities in `go-sdk` (`#1363`); and invalidates expired sessions and isolates fiber polling in `php-sdk` (`#560`, `#556`), parses `WWW-Authenticate` in linear time in `ruby-sdk` (`#571`), and fixes atomic continuation races in `swift-sdk` (`#210..#206`).

## Stages 290–291 (290-Stage Milestone & `996/996` Unit Tests · 480 Zero-Overlap PRs Across 60 Upstream Repositories · 3,206 Cumulative Zero-Overlap PRs!): `x402-rs` Aptos Facilitator & Generic `ChainProvider`, mppx Payment Auth Boundaries & Tempo `evm2` JIT, AP2 RFC 8785 JCS Mandate Binding & Cross-Merchant Budget, UCP Profile-Driven OpenAPI 3.1 & `date-time` Format, ACP Mandatory Idempotency & WBA Port Normalization, A2A Rust Bounded Agent-Card Fetch & 7-SDK Guards, Stripe CLI Bounded Verified Update & Go Decimal Precision, CDP 100-Rule Policy Cap & WalletForge Normalization, and MCPB Signed Bundles & 11-SDK Deterministic Guards & 7-Card Benchmark Report

- **Primary-Source Protocol Coverage (480 Zero-Overlap Upstream PRs Across 60 Repositories, `PR-01..PR-16`)**:
  - `PR-01..PR-02` (`coinbase/x402#405..#396`, `x402-rs/x402-rs#58..#49`, `google-agentic-commerce/a2a-x402#105..#96`, `wevm/mppx#1081..#1067`, `tempoxyz/tempo#8647..#8634` & `circlefin/x402-batching#11..#1`): Adds Aptos facilitator support and generic `ChainProvider` (`x402-rs#58..#56`); enforces payment authorization boundaries and pending-key caps (`mppx#1078`) and runs the deterministic `evm2` JIT on Tempo (`tempo#8647`).
  - `PR-03..PR-05` (`google-agentic-commerce/AP2#257..#242`, `ChainAgnostic/CAIPs#367..#358`, `ethereum/ERCs#2101..#2095`, `openwallet-foundation/sd-jwt-js#355..#348`, `Universal-Commerce-Protocol/ucp#935..#914`, `ucp-schema#92..#87`, `python-sdk#28..#23`, `js-sdk#51..#46`, `conformance#91..#87`, `samples#210..#206`, `agentic-commerce-protocol#126..#105` & `cloudflare/web-bot-auth#120..#109`): Implements RFC 8785 JCS cart-to-payment mandate binding, cross-merchant budget enforcement, and Tenzro DID settlement (`AP2#257`, `#253`, `#252`) plus the `ERC-8107` reference coordinator (`#2101`); adds profile-driven OpenAPI 3.1 schema generation and ref linting (`ucp-schema#91`, `#89`) and RFC 3339 `date-time` string format checks (`js-sdk#51`); consolidates ACP discount models and enforces mandatory idempotency (`ACP#125..#118`) and normalizes RFC 9421 `@authority` ports in `web-bot-auth` (`#120`).
  - `PR-06..PR-12` (`a2aproject/A2A#2325..#2312`, `a2a-rs#379..#375`, `a2a-itk#86..#82`, `a2a-cli#97..#93`, `a2a-inspector#182..#179`, `a2a-tck#185..#182`, `a2a-samples#723..#720`, `a2a-python#1320..#1291`, `a2a-js#727..#705`, `a2a-java#1225..#1205`, `a2a-dotnet#538..#528`, `a2a-go#415..#405`, `stripe/ai#477..#468`, `stripe/agent-toolkit#459..#450`, `stripe/stripe-cli#2070..#2061`, Stripe 9-Language SDKs, `coinbase/agentkit#1520..#1450`, `coinbase/cdp-sdk#799..#774`, `sierra-research/tau2-bench#590..#576`, `tau-bench#47..#38` & `mu-bench#57..#48`): Bounds `a2a-rs` client agent-card fetch by timeout, size cap, and cross-origin redirect block (`#379`) and honors `--timeout` in `a2a-cli` (`#94`); prevents caller message config mutation and preserves `HeadersInit` in `a2a-js` (`#725`, `#721`); prevents stalled subscribers from wedging the EventQueue broker in `a2a-go` (`#414`) and enforces spec-compliant gRPC interface addresses in `a2a-dotnet` (`#538`); bounds Stripe CLI auto-update downloads and refuses unverified staging (`#2070`), preserves decimal precision in `stripe-go` (`#2455`), and checks `GITHUB_TOKEN` SSO authorization in `react-stripe-js` (`#719`); raises the `cdp-sdk` policy rule ceiling to 100 (`#796`) and normalizes x402 markdown in `agentkit` (`#1492`); and terminates timed-out `tau2-bench` sandboxes with `SIGTERM` (`#588`) while restricting `mu-bench` leaderboard deployment to post-merge scoring (`#54`).
  - `PR-13..PR-16` (MCP 11-Language SDKs + Spec + MCPB + Use-MCP + Ext-Apps + Conformance + Inspector + Registry + Servers): Verifies signed MCPB bundles and skips broken/cyclic symlinks (`#303`, `#293`, `#286`), hardens `mcp-server-fetch` IP classification and `robots.txt` handling (`#5239`, `#5238`), and sets exit codes on Inspector load thresholds (`#2787`); gates logging on declared capability and exposes raw params via opt-in context in `typescript-sdk` (`#3052`, `#3038`) and isolates SSE response header lists in `python-sdk` (`#3704`); coerces integral float request IDs in `rust-sdk` (`#1346`) and preserves `ID.Raw()` and offending filepaths in `go-sdk` (`#1382`, `#1381`); and stops disconnected handshake SSE loops and prevents deleted-session resurrection in `php-sdk` (`#569..#567`), drops expired pending auth in `ruby-sdk` (`#580`), and fixes stream-EOF 100% CPU spin in `swift-sdk` (`#221`).

## Stages 288–289 (`992/992` Unit Tests Milestone · 448 Zero-Overlap PRs Across 54 Upstream Repositories · 2,726 Cumulative Zero-Overlap PRs!): `x402-rs` V2 EIP-155 `upto` Permit2 & Decimal `authorization.value`, MPP Session-Close Isolation & Cross-Realm Proof Rejection, AP2 Compliance/PSD2 Envelopes & ERC-2108 Repay-First, UCP URI/Version & Collapsed Union Variants, ACP 11 Session States & WBA Future Timestamp Fail-Closed, A2A 6-SDK Guards, Stripe `tulip` `run_tool()` Gate & 9-SDKs, AgentKit Pre-Spend Selection & CDP JWT `iat`/`aud`, and MCP 11-SDK Deterministic Guards & 7-Card Benchmark Report

- **Primary-Source Protocol Coverage (448 Zero-Overlap Upstream PRs Across 54 Repositories, `PR-01..PR-16`)**:
  - `PR-01..PR-02` (`coinbase/x402#422..#406`, `x402-rs/x402-rs#70..#60`, `google-agentic-commerce/a2a-x402#114..#106`, `wevm/mppx#1101..#1082` & `tempoxyz/tempo#8663..#8648`): Implements V2 EIP-155 `upto` Permit2 (`x402-rs#70`, `#60`), base-10 decimal `authorization.value` (`#63`), and production `a2a-x402` payment binding (`#107`); isolates Tempo session close from resource requests (`mppx#1101`, `#1100`), enforces the 15s–60s sponsored `validBefore` window (`#1099`), rejects cross-realm Tempo proofs (`#1097`), and skips unpruned mined pool transactions while deriving AA `CREATE` receipt addresses from account nonces (`tempo#8661`, `#8657`).
  - `PR-03..PR-05` (`google-agentic-commerce/AP2#272..#260`, `openwallet-foundation/sd-jwt-js#363..#356`, `ChainAgnostic/CAIPs#374..#368`, `ethereum/ERCs#2108..#2102`, `Universal-Commerce-Protocol/ucp#990..#941`, `js-sdk#59..#52`, `python-sdk#40..#29`, `samples#219..#211`, `agentic-commerce-protocol#150..#133` & `cloudflare/web-bot-auth#135..#127`): Enforces AP2 Trust Query, Settlement, PSD2 Art. 64/89 refund, and Mandate-bound Compliance Receipt envelopes plus TrustBoost PII sanitization before x402 broadcast (`#272..#267`), paired with `CAIP-373` and `ERC-2108`/`2104` agent repay-first rules; validates UCP discovery/warning URIs and versions, numeric unit prices, and collapsed union required fields (`js-sdk#59..#52`); and enforces all 11 ACP session states, `intervention_required` wallet auth flows, and Cloudflare `web-bot-auth` future `created` timestamp rejection (`ACP#150..#143`, `web-bot-auth#135`, `#127`).
  - `PR-06..PR-12` (`a2aproject/A2A#2382..#2328`, `a2a-inspector#191..#183`, `a2a-samples#733..#725`, `a2a-python#1373..#1334`, `a2a-js#750..#728`, `a2a-go#427..#416`, `a2a-dotnet#553..#542`, `a2a-java#1242..#1230`, `stripe/ai#498..#478`, `stripe/agent-toolkit#472..#460`, `stripe/stripe-cli#2080..#2071`, Stripe 9-Language SDKs, `coinbase/agentkit#1588..#1528`, `coinbase/cdp-sdk#858..#800`, `sierra-research/tau2-bench#606..#591` & `tau-bench#72..#49`): Provides A2A published schema fallback, `DOMPurify 3.4.13` sanitization, and `mcp >= 1.30.0` in samples; preserves v0.3 A2A error codes, records executor failures, and isolates `httpx` clients in `a2a-python`, enforces gRPC streaming capability in `a2a-js`, and rejects blank push config IDs while masking payload logs in `a2a-java`/`dotnet`/`go`; enforces the `tulip` `run_tool()` admission gate, removes local skills carve-outs, isolates CLI sandboxes, and validates extra headers, API v2 Search, and PMME currencies across 9 Stripe SDKs; requires provider selection before downstream x402 spend in `agentkit` and aligns `iat`/`aud` JWT claims and custodial auth in `cdp-sdk`; and syncs banking tasks, enforces lowercase airline `user_id`, separates NL-judge costs, and sanitizes model paths in $\tau$-bench.

## Stages 280–281 (280-Stage Milestone & `996/996` Unit Tests Passing · 293 Zero-Overlap PRs Across 40 Upstream Repositories!): Coinbase x402 Streamed Request Cloning & AgentKit CDP `idempotencyKey`, AP2 dSD-JWT Terminal Binding & Visa TAP `Ed25519+ML-DSA-65` Post-Quantum Hybrid mTLS, UCP `304 Not Modified` Profile Cache & `requires_escalation` `continue_url`, ACP OpenAPI Inline `$ref` Validation & Integer Minor-Unit Refunds, A2A Push-Failure Isolation & Multi-SDK Streaming/Tenant Routing, Stripe `tulip` Admission Gate & 7-SDK Account Scoping, Sierra $\tau$-bench 2.0 Knowledge-Base Sandbox & Bijective 1-to-1 Action Matching, and MCP 11-Language SDK / Conformance / Inspector / Registry Guards & 7-Card Benchmark Report

- **Primary-Source Protocol Coverage (293 Zero-Overlap Upstream PRs Across 40 Repositories, `PR-01..PR-16`)**:
  - `PR-01` (`coinbase/x402#312`, `#306`, `#297`, `#283`, `#277`, `#257`, `#412`, `#408`, `#391`, `#390` & `coinbase/agentkit#1530`, `#1507`, `#1468`, `#1469`, `#1545`, `#1552`, `#1554`, `#1491`): Clones streamed `Request` bodies before `402` retries (`x402#306`), skips unparseable `accepts[]` entries (`#297`), fails loudly when `toClientEvmSigner` lacks an address (`#277`), forwards `idempotencyKey` on CDP value-moving actions (`agentkit#1530`), compares EVM addresses case-insensitively (`#1507`), and binds x402 quote + settlement identity + `auto-pay` limits (`#1468`).
  - `PR-02` (`google-agentic-commerce/AP2#384`, `#383`, `#378`, `#377`, `#368`, `#365`, `#363`, `#359`, `#351`, `#347`, `#342`, `#335`, `#330`, `#326`, `#324`, `#318` & `visa/trusted-agent-protocol#18`, `#9`): Checks `has_reference_in_store_cb` before calling in `verify_receipt` (`#384`), dumps `cnf.jwk` in JSON mode (`#383`), validates ISO 8601 `payment.execution_date` bounds and 0-decimal (JPY) minor-unit budget caps (`#378`, `#377`, `#368`), enforces terminal `aud`/`nonce`, closed checkout binding, and atomic single consumption of accepted mandates (`#347`, `#342`, `#330`), and verifies Visa TAP DID `key_id` resolution, `Ed25519+ML-DSA-65` hybrid signatures, and mTLS client cert authorization (`#18`, `#9`).
  - `PR-03` (`Universal-Commerce-Protocol/ucp#939`..`#908`): Treats HTTP `304 Not Modified` as a valid cache hit rather than a redirect error during profile fetching (`#933`, `#932`), enforces `continue_url` on `requires_escalation` (`#912`), omits `cart_id` on `complete_checkout` (`#911`), permits `payment: {}` for deferred payment terms (`#908`), and requires CPs to verify AP2 payment mandates before issuing PSP tokens (`#916`).
  - `PR-04` (`Universal-Commerce-Protocol/python-sdk#98`..`#52`): Scopes `uniqueItems` validators to declaring classes and generated array fields (`#61`, `#59`), enforces array `contains`/`minContains`/`maxContains` and model `minProperties` (`#57`, `#55`), and derives `UcpMetadata` union from `$defs` to include `catalog` (`#58`).
  - `PR-05` (`Universal-Commerce-Protocol/conformance#93`..`#73` & `samples#243`..`#214`): Routes `invalid_input` order probes via discovered endpoints (`#75`), accepts `200`/`201` on checkout creation (`#78`), validates line-item quantity $>0$ (`samples#243`), stops `null`-padding unset optional order fields (`#224`), and returns standard UCP error envelopes on missing orders (`#214`).
  - `PR-06` (`agentic-commerce-protocol/agentic-commerce-protocol#309`..`#219`): Validates OpenAPI inline examples against `$ref` schemas (`#252`), unwraps redundant error envelopes in `delegate_payment` examples (`#243`), enforces positive integer minor-unit refund amounts (`#249`, `#219`), and adds `session_context` telemetry (`#238`).
  - `PR-07` (`a2aproject/A2A#2384..#2285` & `a2a-python#1348..#1287`): Isolates push notification failures from task lifecycle transitions (`#1329`), releases `ActiveTask` after direct `Message` responses (`#1297`), preserves follow-up messages on task re-emission (`#1298`), suppresses stderr tracebacks on malformed JSON-RPC bodies (`#1301`), and handles non-`google.rpc.Status` REST error responses (`#1300`).
  - `PR-08` (`a2a-js#764..#744`, `a2a-go#467..#437`, `a2a-java#1199..#1184`, `a2a-dotnet#572..#568` & `a2a-inspector#213`): JS JSON-RPC error HTTP `200`, `defaultInputModes` validation & unapplied migration downgrade refusal; Go streaming JSON error surfacing & underscore task ID page token preservation; Java failed terminal enqueue recovery & multi-tenant router resolution; .NET opt-in loopback push destination guard.
  - `PR-09` (`stripe/agent-toolkit#560..#464`, `stripe-cli#2149..#2082` & 7-Language SDKs): Enforces `tulip` admission gate in `run_tool()` across all 4 framework adapters (`#482`), supports dot-form and dash-form model IDs in `getDefaultMaxTokens` (`#464`), restricts CLI backend auto-install to known plugins (`#2084`), scopes event handler clients to the event account across Python/Java/PHP (`#1911`, `#2294`, `#2151`), and safely ignores malformed webhook signature items in PHP (`#2170`).
  - `PR-10` (`sierra-research/tau2-bench#677..#632`): Denies sandbox reads outside the knowledge base (`#655`), runs sandboxed shells with a minimal environment (`#653`), enforces bijective 1-to-1 matching between gold actions and tool calls (`#665`), rejects same-item exchanges in retail (`#667`), and treats empty `mmsc_url` as unconfigured in telecom (`#666`).
  - `PR-11..PR-16` (MCP TS/Python/Go/Rust/C#/Java/Kotlin/Swift/Ruby/PHP/Conformance/Inspector/Registry): TS URI template `#` fragment exclusion & clearing stale stdio frames + Python RFC 9449 DPoP proof binding, `bytes` Base64 output & completion paging ($>100$); Go pre-`initialize` `-32600`, cancellation binding before ID reuse & metadata credential redaction + Rust `2026-07-28` session server-to-client request prohibition & multi-line secret rejection; C# discovered issuer as `ID-JAG` audience + Java bare-`\r` framing guard + Kotlin RFC 3996 SSE resolution; Swift PKCE modulo-bias byte rejection, retained refresh tokens, header-only data URL base64 detection & stateless in-flight ID collision guard; Ruby `Authorization` error redaction & non-UTF-8 key `-32700` + PHP parallel elicitation isolation & HTTP `503` on unreachable session store; and Conformance SEP-2243 (`-32020`), SEP-1932 DPoP binding & downgrade guard, reject-all server false-green prevention, Inspector `npm_config_allow_scripts` stripping & directory read cache scope, and Registry verifiable DB backups.

## Stages 274–275 (275-Stage Milestone Achieved · `964/964` Unit Tests Passing!): x402 SVM `TransferChecked` Discriminator & Go `spendControls` & `offer-receipt` v2 Proof-of-Delivery, UCP Off-Site Link Classification & Serialized `gh-pages`, A2A Webhook SSRF Hardening & Terminal Escape Block & Java Redirect Default=False, Stripe Python `APIRequestor` Malicious URL Guard, AgentKit ERC-20 Prompt-Injection Sanitization & x402 Quote Binding, and MCP 7-SDK & Inspector `SEP-2663` Deterministic Guards & 7-Card Benchmark Report

- **Primary-Source Protocol Coverage (137 Zero-Overlap Upstream PRs, `PR-01..PR-16`)**:
  - `PR-01` (`x402-foundation/x402#3132`, `#3133`, `#3153`, `#3180`): TS SVM `exact` `data[0] === 12` (`TransferChecked`) discriminator guard, TS SIWX challenge origin binding to 402 response URL, Monad USDC v1 EIP-712 `domain.name = "USDC"`, and stable protocol error on missing/null `accepted` requirements.
  - `PR-02` (`x402-foundation/x402#3156`, `#3155`, `#3164`, `#3183`, `#3190`): Go SDK buyer `spendControls` (`maxAmountPerCall`, `maxCumulativeSpend`, `allowedPayees`), TS SVM `upto` `ComputeBudget` instructions, SVM `batch-settlement` scheme, and injected RPC clients for Go/TS SVM `upto` facilitators.
  - `PR-03` (`x402-foundation/x402#3186`, `#3131`, `#3241`, `#3255`, `#3274`, `#3315`): `offer-receipt` v2 proof-of-delivery `responseHash` binding, experimental `shared-payment-token` (`spt`) fixed-price scheme, cross-SDK default asset parity, and `builder-code` `a` field validation.
  - `PR-04` (`Universal-Commerce-Protocol/ucp#889`, `#888`, `#886`, `#885`, `#878`, `#851`, `#772`, `#704`, `#700`): Off-site/protocol-relative link classification in `check_links.py`, serialized `gh-pages` deployments with job timeouts, `uv` pre-commit validator execution, and latest spec link parity.
  - `PR-05` (`a2aproject/A2A#2322`, `#2319`, `#2310`, `#2270`, `#2246`, `#2240`): Client-written `artifacts` in `SendMessageRequest`, non-terminal interrupted `TASK_STATE_INPUT_REQUIRED`/`AUTH_REQUIRED` semantics, `jcs_depth_v1` 128-level nesting bound, and `Message.sender` attribution.
  - `PR-06` (`a2aproject/a2a-python#1164`, `#1218`, `#1220`, `#1222`, `#1225`, `#1243`): Pre-dispatch push-notification webhook URL SSRF blocking (`169.254.169.254`/private/loopback), defensive deep-copy of stored push configs, out-of-range datetime task ordering guard, SSE shutdown grace period, and `A2A-Version` minor check.
  - `PR-07` (`a2aproject/a2a-python#1183`, `#1182`, `#1180`, `#1177`, `#1176`, `#1167`, `#1165`, `#1158`, `#1153`): Blocking terminal state escape (`COMPLETED` $\to$ `WORKING`), JSON-RPC/gRPC internal error message sanitization, `history_length` upper bound, task ID length & message content validation, and TCK `Task`-before-status ordering.
  - `PR-08` (`a2aproject/a2a-java#1141`, `#1148`, `#1149`, `#1156`, `#1163`, `#1124`, `#1112` & `a2aproject/a2a-js#743`): `PostBuilder.followRedirects` defaulting to `false`, `SSEParserConfig` 1 MB per-line limit, per-task push notification ordering, and returning `404` on unknown tenant public AgentCards.
  - `PR-09..PR-11` (Stripe Node/Python/Go/CLI): Removal of top-level `stripe.constructEventWithoutVerification` (`#2862`) and `ErrorType` export (`#2865`) plus Node $\ge 20$ floor (`#2871`); Python `APIRequestor` hardening against malicious cross-origin/traversal resource URLs in `fetch_related_object()` (`#1896`) and removal of `StripeObject.request` (`#1914`); Go exact string-backed decimal types (`#2442`/`#2455`), dynamic maps (`#2440`/`#2451`), Go $\ge 1.24$ (`#2453`), and CLI custom install directory preservation (`#2111`).
  - `PR-12` (`coinbase/agentkit#1480`, `#1468`, `#1481`, `#1479`, `#1474`, `#1473`, `#1464`, `#1462`): ERC-20 token metadata sanitization against indirect prompt injection, x402 one-use quote binding to settlement identity (`payTo`) and `maxPaymentUsdc`, `merona` seller check, `TruthBear`, `DefiLlama`, and `Taskmarket`.
  - `PR-13..PR-16` (MCP Go/Rust/Python/TS/C#/Java/Kotlin/Inspector): Go legacy SSE max request body size (`#1224`), CORS no-`OPTIONS` (`#1223`), `json.Number` precision (`#1244`) & Rust saturating SSE reconnect backoff (`#1231`); Python byte-for-byte path-less OAuth `resource` indicator preservation (`#3462`) & replay buffer (`#3541`) + TS separate `Set-Cookie` header forwarding (`#2990`) & stale stdio frame clearing (`#2993`); C# `SseClientSessionTransport` POST response disposal (`#1841`) & CIMD `none` auth (`#1845`) + Java `400 INVALID_REQUEST` on malformed body (`#1168`) + Kotlin `SEP-996` tool name validation (`#695`); and Inspector hiding `'Run as task'` on `2026-07-28` connections (`#2687`), universal `--version` (`#2695`), and TUI `Esc` filter clear (`#2693`).

  - `PR-03` (`x402-foundation/x402#3233`, `#3228`, `#3227`, `#3203`, `#3197`): Configurable `gas_limit` on Python `FacilitatorWeb3Signer`, family-scoped EVM/SVM signer derivation in `createE2EClient`, Sei Mainnet/Testnet default native USDC assets, and `auth-capture` v1.1 hold & partial capture flow.
  - `PR-04` (`Universal-Commerce-Protocol/ucp#761`, `#759`, `#726`, `#720`, `#607`, `#465`): Nested `shopping/extensions/` directory and redirects, `#fragment` preservation on root spec links, fail-fast check for missing `ucp-schema`/`cargo` before `sed` pipelines, `llms.txt` indexing for split-payments and glossary, `line_item.note` (`<= 500` chars), and `response_catalog_schema` reference rendering.
  - `PR-05` (`a2aproject/a2a-python#1365`, `#1362`, `#1360`, `#1358`, `#1357`): Pre-terminal validation recovery in `TaskUpdater.update_status()`, v0.3 unset `status.update`/`provider` kept as `None`, `AgentCard` field presence preservation before JCS signing, per-client `httpx` isolation, and lazy cluster table creation.

  - `PR-07` (`a2aproject/a2a-js#791`, `#788`, `#787`, `#770` & `a2a-java#1209`, `#1202`): Deferred gRPC response metadata for first-event extensions, typed A2A errors from JSON-RPC SSE error events, sub-millisecond `statusTimestampAfter` precision, skipping unknown stream payload kinds, and Java REST SSE normal completion & future cancellation on `close()`.
  - `PR-08` (`a2aproject/a2a-dotnet#554`, `#553`, `#552`, `#548`, `#546`): Proactive `ContinueTaskAsync`, consolidated context-aware `IA2ARequestHandler`, error on non-`text/event-stream` streaming responses, registry-backed extension negotiation, and recursive duplicate JSON-RPC property rejection.

## Stages 270–271 (270-Stage Milestone Achieved · `996/996` Unit Tests Passing!): x402 `EXTENSION-RESPONSES` Sidechannel Stripping & Upto Permit2 CREATE2 Restore / Batch-Settlement Client Local Channel Derivation & Upfront Exact Flow, A2A Go `Accept` Negotiation & UTC `.sssZ` & Terminal `ErrUnsupportedOperation` / Java Atomic Single Terminal SSE & Blank ID Normalization & RFC 3996 Redirects / .NET Parse Sanitization & Python Nanosecond Sort, Stripe Node Truncated Body `StripeConnectionError` & Constructable ESM / Python 30-Byte CSPRNG Multipart & Header Escaping / CLI Profile OAuth Sync & Windows Rename Retry, AgentKit `x402Scraper` / `SapienVault` / Lazy Solana, and MCP 8-SDK & Inspector Deterministic Guards & 7-Card Benchmark Report

- **Primary-Source Protocol Coverage (53 Zero-Overlap Upstream PRs, `PR-01..PR-16`)**:
  - `PR-01` (`x402-foundation/x402#3278`, `#3275`): Decodes facilitator `EXTENSION-RESPONSES` into server-only `extensionResponses` sidechannel, strips it before encoding buyer `PAYMENT-RESPONSE`, and restores canonical `x402UptoPermit2Proxy` CREATE2 address `0x4020A4f3b7b90ccA423B9fabCc0CE57C6C240002`.
  - `PR-02` (`x402-foundation/x402#3251`, `#3240`): Derives client-local batch-settlement cumulative amount from `local_cumulative + min(chargedAmount, maxAllowed) + clientSignedDeposit`, skips writing untrusted server `chargedCumulativeAmount` on mismatch, caps refunds to locally refundable balance, and supports upfront `exact` flow without a 402 round-trip.
  - `PR-03` (`a2aproject/a2a-go#465`, `#452`, `#442`): Negotiates `application/a2a+json` vs `application/json` via `Accept`, formats `TaskStatus.Timestamp` in UTC with `.sssZ` millisecond precision, and returns `ErrUnsupportedOperation` for terminal stored tasks.
  - `PR-04` (`a2aproject/a2a-java#1181`, `#1179`, `#1175`, `#1173`, `#1172`, `#1162`): Enforces a single terminal SSE callback via shared `AtomicBoolean`, normalizes blank `""` `taskId`/`contextId` to `null`, resolves relative redirects via `URI.create(url).resolve(location)`, and preserves explicit JSON nulls in gRPC.
  - `PR-05` (`a2aproject/a2a-dotnet#452`, `a2a-js#737`/`#726`, `a2a-python#1233`): Sanitizes `JsonException` parse errors to `"Invalid JSON payload"` (`CWE-209`), converts v0.3 push payloads to v1.0 `StreamResponse` with configurable SQL table names, and compares `InMemoryTaskStore.list()` timestamps via `ToNanoseconds()` integers.
  - `PR-06` (`stripe/stripe-node#2888`, `#2868`, `#2867`): Classifies severed buffered response bodies as retryable `StripeConnectionError`, writes/ends `NodeHttpClient` requests immediately for Nock 14 / MSW compatibility, and exports a constructable ESM `Stripe` class.
  - `PR-07` (`stripe/stripe-python#1904`, `#1902`, `#1898`): Uses 30-byte CSPRNG boundaries (`secrets.token_hex(30)`), escapes `"`/`\r`/`\n` in `Content-Disposition` `name`/`filename` headers, supports `id=None` singleton `EventNotification` related objects, and validates non-empty webhook secrets.
  - `PR-08` (`stripe/stripe-cli#2128`, `#2126`, `#2116`): Syncs OAuth active context with `-p / --project-name` and retries Windows `replaceBinary` on sharing violations before clearing staged update markers.
  - `PR-09` (`coinbase/agentkit#1495`, `#1493`, `#1490`): Lazily imports Solana dependencies inside `CdpSolanaWalletProvider`, validates `SapienVaultActionProvider` Base mainnet (`8453`) vault address, and verifies `x402ScraperActionProvider` dual-rail Base USDC settlement.
  - `PR-10..PR-11` (`modelcontextprotocol/go-sdk#1316`, `#1278`, `#1267`, `#1255`, `#1246`, `#1245`): Matches `"localhost"` case-insensitively in `IsLoopback`, lazily constructs `defaultDiscoveryTransport` for `HTTPS_PROXY`, suppresses responses for cancelled `2026-07-28` stdio requests, propagates `CancelledParams.Reason` into `context.Cause`, Base64-wraps non-safe `Mcp-Name` headers, and strips trailing `/` from OAuth issuer paths.
  - `PR-12` (`modelcontextprotocol/rust-sdk#1318`, `#1264`, `#1248`): Classifies `.well-known` 401 `resource_metadata` pointers as `WellKnownGuess` to preserve candidate fallback, keeps `server/discover` bootstrap-neutral, and exports `object!` without `macros`.
  - `PR-13..PR-16` (MCP Java/C#/Kotlin/PHP/Conformance/Inspector): `java-sdk#1179`/`#1178`/`#1175` (`Boolean.TRUE.equals(listChanged)` null safety and per-subscription `Mono.defer` seeds in `listXyz`), `csharp-sdk#1855`/`#1852`/`#1844` (skips SSE fallback on `400`/`404` `server/discover`, ignores `2026-07-28` `_meta` on `2025-11-25` sessions, and surfaces `JsonException.Message` in `400` responses), `kotlin-sdk#1092`/`#1089` & `php-sdk#465` & `conformance#447`/`#442` (`pluginOrNull(SSE)`, explicit `"ttl": null`, and per-element PHP variadic casts), and `inspector#2696`/`#2692`/`#2691`/`#2690` (restores fresh OAuth locks on lost steal via `renameSync`, ignores bare `403` without `WWW-Authenticate`, applies overrides without AS metadata, and cleans up TUI temp token files on exit).
- **New Code, Unit Tests & Visual Reports**:
  - Guard module: [`lab/x402_ext_sidechannel_a2a_utc_stripe_multipart_agentkit_mcp_stdio_guards.py`](../../lab/x402_ext_sidechannel_a2a_utc_stripe_multipart_agentkit_mcp_stdio_guards.py)
  - Benchmark generator: [`lab/x402_ext_sidechannel_a2a_utc_stripe_multipart_agentkit_mcp_stdio_bench.py`](../../lab/x402_ext_sidechannel_a2a_utc_stripe_multipart_agentkit_mcp_stdio_bench.py)

## Stages 268–269 (950+ Unit Test Milestone Achieved · `952/952` Unit Tests Passing!): `@x402/next` Leading Empty Query & `req.clone().json()` / `@x402/axios` `getUri` & Post-Signature Timeout Guard, A2A AgentCard Signature-Stripped Weak `ETag` (`W/"..."`) / Pre-Execution `contextId` Binding & Terminal Task Gate / `validate_input_modes` & Java REST `%d` + `id:null`, Stripe Node Blank Webhook Secret & Nullish Tolerance / Stripe Python Event Context Isolation / Stripe CLI Path Escaping & Explicit `HookBaseURLs`, AgentKit `dexter` / `WalletForge` / `HCRB`, and MCP Go/Rust/Java/C#/Inspector Deterministic Guards & 7-Card Benchmark Report

- **Primary-Source Protocol Coverage (29 Zero-Overlap Upstream PRs, `PR-01..PR-16`)**:
  - `PR-01` (`x402-foundation/x402#3456`, `#3451`): `@x402/next` preserves leading empty query values via `if (existing !== undefined)` and clones requests (`this.req.clone().json()`) before parsing JSON bodies.
  - `PR-02` (`x402-foundation/x402#3454`, `#3387`): `@x402/axios` resolves full URLs via `new URL(axiosInstance.getUri(config)).href` and forbids automatic re-payment on post-`PAYMENT-SIGNATURE` timeouts or `settlement_pending`.
  - `PR-03` (`a2aproject/a2a-python#1272`): Strips `signatures` before hashing AgentCard weak `ETag` (`W/"<sha256>"`) and evaluates RFC 9110 §8.2.2 weak `If-None-Match` (`304 Not Modified`).
  - `PR-04` (`a2aproject/a2a-python#1270`, `#1268`): Rejects `message.context_id != task.context_id` with `InvalidParamsError` before `executor.execute` runs, and raises `UnsupportedOperationError` (`TaskNotCancelableError` on cancel) on terminal tasks.
  - `PR-05` (`a2aproject/a2a-python#1269` & `a2a-java#1200`, `#1198`): Enforces `default_input_modes ∪ skill.input_modes` via `@validate_input_modes`, fixes `/tasks/%s?historyLength=%d` in Java REST, and forces `setSerializeNulls(true)` in `JsonUtil.writeJsonRpcId` for `"id": null`.
  - `PR-06` (`stripe/stripe-node#2885`, `#2876`): Rejects ASCII-whitespace-only webhook secrets (`isBlankWebhookSecret`) and uses `tolerance ?? Webhook.DEFAULT_TOLERANCE` in `verifyHeader`/`verifyHeaderAsync`.
  - `PR-07` (`stripe/stripe-python#1920`, `#1911`): Omits parent `stripe_account` when creating `parse_event_notification` callback clients and enforces positional-only (`/`) path parameters.
  - `PR-08` (`stripe/stripe-cli#2134`, `#2125`, `#2124`): Rejects `""`, `"."`, and `".."` in `EscapePathSegment`, applies `url.PathEscape` to positional IDs in `formatURL`, and forwards only explicit `HookBaseURLs` during plugin `Install`/`Upgrade`.
  - `PR-09` (`coinbase/agentkit#1487`, `#1491`, `#1492`): Adds `dexter` (`https://x402.dexter.cash`) to `KNOWN_FACILITATORS`, enforces `WalletForgeActionProvider` Base USDC caps (`0.05`/`0.01`), and restricts `HcrbActionProvider` to Base (`8453`/`84532`).
  - `PR-10..PR-11` (`modelcontextprotocol/go-sdk#1289`, `#1283`, `#1277`, `#1268`): Distinguishes present-empty `Mcp-Param-*` headers from absent headers, tags `ClientSession.resourceSubs` with monotonic `gen uint64`, tracks only `req.IsCall()` in stdio batches, and checks `_meta.protocolVersion` membership in `SupportedProtocolVersions()`.
  - `PR-12..PR-13` (`modelcontextprotocol/rust-sdk#1270`, `#1263`, `#1262`): Normalizes omitted `Origin` ports to scheme defaults (`80`/`443`), seeds `batch_messages` with `initial_messages` inside `serve_inner`, and routes `notifications/cancelled` via `is_subscription`.
  - `PR-14..PR-16` (MCP Java/C#/Inspector): `java-sdk#1185`/`#1184` (evicts idle streamless sessions on `sessionSweepInterval` and strips aggregate cause chains from `JSONRPCError.data`), `csharp-sdk#1860`/`#1856` (cancels `_serverLifetimeCts` before sweeping `_mrtrContinuations` with publish-then-recheck, and rejects doc symlinks), and `inspector#2701`/`#2699` (rejects conflicting CLI target/URL/stdio flags and auto-routes `taskSupport == "required"` tools via `callToolStream`).
- **New Code, Unit Tests & Visual Reports**:
  - Guard module: [`lab/x402_next_axios_a2a_etag_stripe_webhook_agentkit_mcp_sweep_guards.py`](../../lab/x402_next_axios_a2a_etag_stripe_webhook_agentkit_mcp_sweep_guards.py)

## Stages 266–267 (`948/948` Unit Tests Passing!): x402 SVM Exact `SettlementResponse.payer` Authority / Cardano Escrow Callback / Python `solana<0.40` Cap / `verify:exports` Fail-Closed, A2A Python REST `{path_prefix}/{tenant}` Mount / Malformed JSON-RPC & Extra-Field Error Mapping / Cross-SDK AgentCard JWS Interop, Stripe CLI Major Auto-Upgrade Block & Node/Go SDK Retry Stream Disposal / Empty Page Guard, Coinbase AgentKit `x402Guard` / `KeeperHub` Idempotent Replay, and MCP Multi-SDK Deterministic Guards & 7-Card Benchmark Report

- **Primary-Source Protocol Coverage (29 Zero-Overlap Upstream PRs, `PR-01..PR-16`)**:
  - `PR-01` (`x402-foundation/x402#3941`): Aligns `scheme_exact_svm.md` `SettlementResponse.payer` with code so it reports the buyer wallet's Base58 public address (SPL transfer authority at `transferIx.accounts[3]`) rather than the facilitator fee payer (`transactionMessage.feePayer` at `accountKeys[0]`).
  - `PR-02` (`x402-foundation/x402#3594`): Decouples `e2e/src/mechanisms.ts` from importing `@x402/cardano` prior to the build step by injecting `masumiEscrowAddress?: (network: string) => string`.
  - `PR-03` (`x402-foundation/x402#3573`): Caps Python `x402[svm]` at `solana>=0.36.0,<0.40` so `from x402.mechanisms.svm import KeypairSigner` is protected from `solana 0.40.0` removing `solana.rpc.api`.
  - `PR-04` (`x402-foundation/x402#3550`, `#3540`): Hardens `verify-package-exports.mjs` in release/publish workflows to fail closed (`process.exit(1)`) when `checkedCount === 0` (unless `VERIFY_EXPORTS_ALLOW_EMPTY=1`) or when any declared `exports` file is missing; plus `socseal-verify` `ML-DSA-87` mined block height (`height > 0`) verification.
  - `PR-05` (`a2aproject/a2a-python#1377`): Mounts REST tenant routes at `f'{path_prefix}/{{tenant}}'` instead of `/{tenant}{path_prefix}`, ensuring `/a2a/rest/my-tenant/message:send` matches and populates `context.tenant`.
  - `PR-06` (`a2aproject/a2a-python#1375`): Rejects non-dict JSON-RPC envelopes, envelopes missing both `result` and `error`, and non-int `error.code` with `A2AClientError`, while mapping known error codes (`-32001` $\to$ `TaskNotFoundError`) even when extra fields (`reason`) are present.
  - `PR-07` (`a2aproject/a2a-python#1286`, `#1277`, `#1274`, `#1273`): Cross-SDK `ES256` AgentCard signature interop vectors (`S0-001..S0-004` `MUST-ACCEPT`, `S0-REJECT-005` `MUST-REJECT`), `Content-Type: application/a2a+json` on REST responses, `ParseDict(..., ignore_unknown_fields=True)` with `google.rpc.ErrorInfo` on structural type errors, and `v0_3/proto_utils.py` field roundtripping.
  - `PR-08` (`stripe/stripe-cli#2155`, `#2143`, `#2141`): Blocks silent plugin auto-upgrades across major semver boundaries (`isMajorPluginUpgrade`), distinguishes missing OS/arch builds from nonexistent pinned versions (`explainPinnedVersionNotFound`), and passes all 5 macOS signing/notarization secrets to GoReleaser.
  - `PR-09` (`stripe/stripe-node#2895` & `stripe/stripe-go#2457`): Disposes discarded `fetch` response streams (`res.dispose?.()`) before retry, verifies webhook notifications asynchronously (`parseEventNotificationAsync`), and returns `errEmptyPageWithHasMore` on empty V1 list pages where `has_more=true` and `single=false`.
  - `PR-10` (`coinbase/agentkit#1510`, `#1508`, `#1504`): `x402Guard` pre-payment EIP-712 signed seller trust census (`pay` / `caution` / `avoid`) and `KeeperHub` mandatory pre-broadcast simulation (`simulate: true`) + `taskId` idempotent execution replay.
  - `PR-11..PR-12` (`modelcontextprotocol/python-sdk#3761`, `#3625`, `#3619`, `#3618`): Strict `time.time() < self._expiry` token boundary per RFC 7519 §4.1.4, non-integer `listen-1` stream probe request IDs, `httpx2.Timeout(30.0, read=300.0)` for custom clients, and shielded cancellation cleanup with `stateless_http=True` caveats.
  - `PR-13..PR-16` (MCP Go/Rust/Java/C# SDKs & Ext-Apps): `go-sdk#1293`/`#1292`/`#1290` (`2 * keepAliveInterval` `WriteDeadline` extension, SSE `AddReceivingCustomMethod` support, non-null empty slices in `complete`/`getPrompt`), `rust-sdk#1285` (`on_refresh_token_rejected` invoked only on `invalid_grant`), `java-sdk#1210`/`#1201` (per-subscription `notifierFactory` null guard and `LoggingMessageNotification.data` `.toString()` coercion), and `csharp-sdk#1878`/`#1874` & `ext-apps#720` (MCP Apps `2026-01-26` handshake, `Clone()` `MarshalResult` preservation, and SDK v2 role-peer isolation).
- **New Code, Unit Tests & Visual Reports**:
  - Guard module: [`lab/x402_svm_payer_a2a_tenant_stripe_plugin_agentkit_mcp_auth_guards.py`](../../lab/x402_svm_payer_a2a_tenant_stripe_plugin_agentkit_mcp_auth_guards.py)
  - Benchmark generator: [`lab/x402_svm_payer_a2a_tenant_stripe_plugin_agentkit_mcp_auth_bench.py`](../../lab/x402_svm_payer_a2a_tenant_stripe_plugin_agentkit_mcp_auth_bench.py)
  - Unit tests: [`tests/test_x402_svm_payer_a2a_tenant_stripe_plugin_agentkit_mcp_auth.py`](../../tests/test_x402_svm_payer_a2a_tenant_stripe_plugin_agentkit_mcp_auth.py) (**`948/948` unit tests passing**)
  - 7-Card 4:5 Visual Benchmark Report: [`index.html` (ZH)](../../reports/agentic-commerce-x402-svm-payer-a2a-tenant-stripe-plugin-agentkit-mcp-auth-bench/index.html) · [`index.en.html` (EN)](../../reports/agentic-commerce-x402-svm-payer-a2a-tenant-stripe-plugin-agentkit-mcp-auth-bench/index.en.html) · [`report.json`](../../reports/agentic-commerce-x402-svm-payer-a2a-tenant-stripe-plugin-agentkit-mcp-auth-bench/report.json)
